pṛ0 3a) vidhāya japānaṃtaraṃpunaḥṣaḍaṃgaṃkṛtvāguhyātiguhyetipūrvoktamaṃtreṇajapaṃsamarpyapraṇamet kecitmaṃtramenamekonaviṃ

pṛ0 34a) dayeśrīvarāhāyadevatāyainamaḥ itivinyasyamamasarvābhīṣṭasiddhayejapeviniyoga itikṛtāṃjaliruktā ekaśṛṃ

pṛ0 34ba) paṃsamarpyapraṇamet itivārāhamaṃtraḥ athadvitīyovarāhamaṃtraḥ huṃityekākṣaramaṃtreṇaprāṇāyāmavidhāya śirasi

pṛ0 54a) teṛṣyādikaṃvinyasyajapaṃdevāyasamarpyapraṇamet itidvātraṃśākṣaraḥkṛṣṇamaṃtraḥ athaṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ kṛṣṇamaṃtraḥ śrīṃ

ṣeṣusthitānāṃgrahāṇāṃśubhāśubhaphālmanisārasaṃgrahe triṣaḍāyagatāḥpāpā śubhākeṃdratrikoṇagāḥ dīkṣāyāṃ

pṛ0 78a) tuśubhāḥsarveraṃdhrasthāḥsarvanāśakāḥ āyaikādaśasthānaṃ lagnacaturthasaptamadaśasthānānāṃkeṃdrasaṃjñāḥ paṃcamana
vamasthānayostrikoṇasaṃjñā sūryakujaśanirāhuketupāpāyutavudhāḥ kṣīṇaḥśaśīcapāpāḥ gurubhṛgusutapāpa
rahitavudhāḥ pūrṇaścaṃdraśca śubhāḥ raṃdhramaṣṭamasthānaṃ sarve navagrahāityarthaḥ athadīkṣākālaḥ tatraśaivāgame adhomu
kheśubhebhecacaṃdraśuddhauviśeṣataḥ kṛṣṇetārāvalaṃkuryātsvanāmādiviciṃtanaṃ dharmmadaḥprātakālaḥsyātsaṃgavorājya
daḥ smṛtaḥ madhyāhnesarvasiddhisyātsāyāhnomadhyamobhavet rajanīmaṃtradānetuniṣiddhādeśikottamaiḥ divāsarvaṃ
prakurvītasiddhidaṃparamaṃsmṛtaṃ adhomukhanakṣatrāṇitumūlāgreyamadyāddhidevabharaṇīsārppāṇipūrvātrayaṃ jyoti
rvidbhiradhomukhaṃhinavakaṃ bhānāmidaṃkīrtitaṃ vāpīkūpataḍāgāgartaparikhākhātonidhesadhṛtiḥ kṣepodyūtavila
pūveśagaṇitāraṃbhāḥprasiddhaṃtica dvidevaṃviśākakhāāgneyaṃkṛtikāsārpaṃaśleṣā athadīkṣākālaviśeṣaḥ 
jñānārṇave matrādyāraṃbhaṇaṃkuryātgrahaṇecaṃdrasūryayoḥ grahaṇādevadeveśikālaḥsaptadināvadhi tathākulamūlā
vatāre pavitraparvadeveśidāmane(damanāropaṇe)vāśubhedinekālavacanikatarvyāgrahaṇecaṃdrasūryayoḥ puṇyatīrthekurukṣetrede
vīpīṭhacatuṣṭaye tathāratnasāgare satīrtherkavidhugrāsetaṃtudāmanaparvarṇoḥ maṃtradīkṣāṃprakurvāṇomāsakṣāṃdīnna

pṛ0 78ba) śodhayet atrayadyapisūryasomagrahaṇayodīkṣāyāṃsamānakālatoktātathāpigrahaṇaṃpraśaṃsākānivacanāni
sūryagrahaṇaparāṇyevajñeyāni sūryagrahaṇakāletunānyadanveṣitaṃbhavet sūryagrahagrahaṇakālenasomonānyaḥka
dācana atrayadyatkṛtaṃsarvamanaṃtaphaladaṃbhavet namāsatithivārādiśodhanaṃsūryaparvaṇi dadātīṣṭaṃcagṛhṇīyāta
sminkālegurornaraḥ siddhirbhavatimaṃtrasyavinābhyāsenavegatiticiṃtāmaṇikālikodbhavayoḥ sūryagrahaṇasyai
vavidhāyakavacanadarśanāt caṃdragrahecayādīkṣāyādīkṣāvanacāriṇāṃ janakasyatuyādīkṣādāridryaṃsadyajanma
svitiyoginītaṃtre caṃdragrahaṇedīkṣāniṣedhavacanadarśanātprāguktarajanīmaṃtradānaniṣedhavacanācceti sūryoparā
gakālopyuparāgasādhāraṇatayānātyaṃtaṃpraśasnitijñeyaḥ kiṃtu caṃdrasūryagrahetīrthesiddhakṣetreśivālaye maṃtra
mātrasyakathanamupadeśaḥsucyate itikolikodbhavavacanāt maṃtrāṃtareṣuvidhivadīkṣitānāṃkautukārthamanya
maṃtragrahaṇechācedbhavatitadānijagurorevasakāśāccaṃdrasūryagrahaṇakālopyupadeśatomaṃtragrahaṇenadoṣāvad 
itidīkṣākālaḥ athajapamālāsaubhāgyaratnākare sātuputrajīvaśaṃkhapravālaratnasphaṭikamuktāphalapadmavīja
rajatasvarṇakuśagraṃthirudrākṣānyatamanirmitāṣṭotaraśatacatuḥpaṃcāśatsaptaviṃśatisaṃkhyātairvījairyuktāsameru

pṛ0 79a) kākāryā athamālāsaṃskārakālastatraiva viṣṇordvādaśyāḥpūrvāhṇaḥśakteraṣṭamīnavamīcaturdaśīrātriḥ śivasyatra
yodaśīdivā gaṇeśasyacaturdaśīdivā sūryasyasaptamīdivā athamālāyāḥ sūtrāṇitatraiva viṣṇoḥpadmasūtraṃkāryā
sasūtraṃvādevyāḥpaṭṭasūtraṃ śivasyorṇābhavaṃvālkalaṃvā sūryagaṇeśayoḥkārpāsasūtraṃpaṭṭasūtraṃvā kāryāsasūtraṃ
cet brahmaṇīkṛtaṃsvasamānavarṇayoṣitkṛtaṃvātriguṇīkṛtaṃkuryāt anyeṣusūtreṣuyathecchamevasthaulyamānaṃ a
thamālāsaṃskāraḥ tatragomūtragomayadugdhadadhighṛtakuśajalātmakaṃpaṃcagavyaṃhomārthaṃvṛtaṃtilānsaṃpādyaprātaḥ
kṛtanityakṛtakṛtabhojanaḥsādhakorātrausūtraṃmaṇīṃścapṛthakpaṃcagavyenajalaiścaprakṣālyanavasupippaladale
ṣupadmākāraraciteṣusūtraṃmaṇīṃścasaṃsthāpyateṣupraṇavaṃhrikāramātṛkāṃcavinyasyamaṇīn sūtraṃcagaṃdhādibhiḥsaṃ
pūjyasthaṃḍilādāvagniṃsaṃsthāpyayathāśaktisaghṛtaistilaiḥsadyādipaṃcamaṃtrairhutvāhomāśaktaujapaṃvidhāyasūtremu
kheṣumukhaṃsaṃyojyayanmaṇīnāropyaikaikamaṇimadhyevrahmagraṃthiṃvidhāyagopuchākāreṇagraṃthayitvāsarvataḥsthūlaṃsa
jātīyamekaṃmaṇiṃsūtradvayamekīkṛtyameruṃgrathitvā sadyojātaṃprapadyāsisadyojātāyavainamaḥ bhavebhavenādibha
vebhajasvamāṃbhavodbhavāyanamaḥ itimaṃtreṇapaṃcagavyenaśītalajalenacaprakṣālya oṃvāmadevāyanamojyeṣṭāyanamo

pṛ0 79ba) rudrāyanamaḥ kālāyanamaḥ kalavikaraṇāyanamobalavikaraṇāyanamovalapramathanāyanamaḥ sarvabhūtadamanāya
namomanonmanāyanamaḥ itimaṃtreṇacaṃdanāgurukarpūrakuṃkumairvilipya oṃaghorebhyothaghorebhyoghoraghoratare
bhyaḥsarvataḥsarvasarvebhyonamasteastarudrarūpebhyaḥ ityagurūśīraśarkarāguggulumadhucaṃdanaghṛtaidhūpayitvā oṃta
tpuruṣāyavidmahemahādevāyadhīmahi tannorudraḥpracodayāt itigaṃdhacaṃdanakastūrīkuṃkumakarpūrailepayet ta
taḥoṃīśānaḥsarvavidyānāmīśvaraḥsarvabhūtānāṃbrahmādhipativrāhmaṇodhipatirvrahmāśivomeastusadāśivoṃ 
itimaṃtreṇaprativījaṃśataṃśatamabhimaṃtrayet meruṃcānanāghorebhyoityanenāpicābhimaṃtrayet tataścāvāhanādi
navamudrāḥpradarśyaprāguktaipaṃcamaṃtraiḥ prativījaṃpaṃcopacāraiḥ pūjayitvātayāmālaprājapaṃkuryāditi ayaṃpratiṣṭā
prakārosadrākṣāṇāmevanānyeṣāṃ anyeṣāṃpratiṣṭāprakārastu grathitāṃmālāṃkutracitpātresaṃsthāpyatasyāṃgaṇeśa
sūryaviṣṇuśivadurgāḥpṛthakpṛthak āvāhyapaṃcopacopacāraiḥsaṃpūjyahauṃ itimaṃtreṇapaṃcagavyenikṣipyapunaḥ
stasmādudhṛtyaśītalajalenikṣipyaprakṣānyacaṃdanakarpūrakastūrīkuṃkumakarpūrāgurubhiranulipyatasyāṃhauṃmaṃtrama
ṣṭottaraśataṃjapitvāsūryādinavagrahāniṃdrādidikpālāṃścasaṃpūjyasaghṛtaistilairyathāśaktimūlamaṃtreṇahutvā


pṛ0 ) maṃtracaṃdrakāsūcisameta
patre 85 śloka 2700
